exist within verbose traces.\n\n\\paragraph{Density and cross-model robustness.}\n\\texttt{exception\\_only}'s cross-model range is ${<}$1pp vs.\\ ${\\sim}$9pp for \\texttt{loop\\_only} (Table~\\ref{tab:cross_model}), though with $N{=}3$ models density cannot be disentangled from signal type.\nA corroborating negative result: label-only loss on DeepSeek full traces (91.7\\% truncated) causes model collapse (28.5\\%, eval loss $\\to$ NaN), confirming content density, not loss function, is the binding constraint.\nVOTA's tokenizer-dependent truncation vulnerability (Appendix~\\ref{sec:tokenizer_analysis}) further favors \\texttt{exception\\_only} for cross-model deployment.\n\n\\paragraph{Exception events capture test verdict signals, not just crashes.}\nAlthough 55.5\\% of failures are silent logic errors, \\texttt{exception\\_only} achieves 100\\% fail recall because test assertions generate \\texttt{AssertionError} events, confirming ``exception events'' encompasses both crashes and assertion failures.\n\n\\paragraph{Alternative explanations and control evidence.}\nThree alternative explanations for the \\texttt{exception\\_only} advantage merit discussion.\n\\emph{Length asymmetry}: fail cases contain ${\\sim}$150-token tracebacks while pass cases contain only a short ``no exception'' message, so models could exploit a length heuristic. To control for this, we padded all \\texttt{exception\\_only} inputs to 2{,}048 tokens matching \\texttt{full\\_trace} length; accuracy remained 99.83\\%, identical to the unpadded condition, ruling out length as a confound.\n\\emph{Format-marker familiarity}: removing all structural markers (\\texttt{[ERROR]}, \\texttt{[Line~N]}) reduced accuracy by only 2.18pp (97.65\\%), indicating markers contribute minimally (${\\sim}$9\\% of the 24pp gap). However, this does not fully control for familiarity with exception-message formats in pretraining corpora.\n\\emph{Positional concentration}: \\S\\ref{sec:trace_length} confirms diagnostic information concentrates in the trace tail. We cannot rule out that positional concentration, rather than information density per se, explains part of the advantage; disentangling position from density requires position-controlled experiments left to future work.\nThese controls narrow but do not eliminate the set of alternative explanations. We therefore treat information density as one candidate mechanism among several, supported by converging evidence from gradient masking (\\S\\ref{sec:density}), cross-dataset transfer (\\S\\ref{sec:additional}), and the controls above.\n\n\\paragraph{Information density and learning dynamics.}\nTraining dynamics confirm the density effect: \\texttt{exception\\_only} converges to 99.33\\% within 50 steps and saturates at 99.83\\% by step 150, while \\texttt{full\\_trace} with label-only loss oscillates between 82--89\\% across all 444 steps without stable convergence (Appendix~\\ref{sec:training_dynamics}, Figure~\\ref{fig:training_dynamics}).\nSeed variance negatively correlates with input density (Table~\\ref{tab:ablation}), confirming compact inputs stabilize learning.\n",
    "userModified": false
  },

{
  "type": "queue-operation",

  "timestamp": "2026-05-25T09:07:18.073Z",
  "sessionId": "831b75c5-679f-4a8f-8a75-2b5724408732",
  "content": "[飞书用户 用户]: 📑 论文自检任务：附录内容引用完整性检查\n\n请检查论文附录（Appendix）的所有内容是否在正文里都有准确、合理的引用。\n\nAI 写作的高频问题：附录里塞了一堆补充材料（额外实验、超参表、prompt 模板、proof、failure case），但正文从头到尾没有一句话指向它们。这种\"孤儿附录内容\"对 reviewer 来说等于不存在 —— 既浪费篇幅，也让附录显得拼凑。\n\nACL 政策也明确：附录是 supplementary 性质的延伸，必须由正文驱动读者去看。完全没被引用的附录章节会被视为低质量补充材料。\n\n---\n\n自检要求：\n\n1. 逐项盘点附录\n   把附录里的每一个 section / subsection / table / figure / algorithm / proof / prompt 都列出来。常见类型：\n   - Appendix A: Implementation details / Training details\n   - Appendix B: Additional results / Ablations\n   - Appendix C: Hyperparameter search\n   - Appendix D: Prompt templates\n   - Appendix E: Proofs\n   - Appendix F: Failure cases / Error analysis / Qualitative examples\n   - Appendix G: Dataset construction details\n\n2. 逐项验证引用\n   对每一项，在正文里搜：是否有至少一句话指向它？引用形式应该是：\n   - \"... (see Appendix A for full hyperparameters)\"\n   - \"... we defer the proof to Appendix B\"\n   - \"... Table 5 in Appendix C reports the breakdown\"\n   - \"... Figure 7 (Appendix D) visualizes ...\"\n\n   不合格的\"引用\"：\n   ❌ 只在 paper 末尾甩一句 \"See appendix for more details\"（没指向具体 section）\n   ❌ 在 Limitations 或 Conclusion 才提一次（应该在内容相关的章节就引出）\n   ❌ 引用了但没说明为什么读者要去看（\"see Appendix B\" 没下文）\n\n3. 修复策略（按优先级）\n   - 优选：在最相关的正文章节加一句自然的引用。例如训练细节 → 在 Experimental Setup 末尾：\"For full training details including learning rate schedule and gradient clipping, see Appendix A.\"\n   - 次选：如果实在找不到合适位置插入引用，且该附录内容对核心 claim 不必要，直接删除附录内容（不要保留无引用的\"装饰性附录\"）\n   - 不要做：在某个章节最后硬塞一行 \"See Appendix X.\" 当作补丁——这不算合理引用\n\n4. 引用质量原则\n   每条引用应该让 reviewer 立刻明白：\n   - 为什么他要去翻那里（解决什么疑问 / 验证什么 claim）\n   - 能在那里找到什么（具体是表、图、proof 还是定性例子）\n\n---\n\n完成后请汇报：\n\n(a) 附录有多少个 section / subsection？分别是什么内容？\n(b) 哪些原本没有正文引用？现在加在了哪个章节哪一句话？\n(c) 是否有附录内容被你判定为\"无必要\"而删除？删掉了哪些？"
}

_source line 1021 · 2026-05-25T09:07:18.239Z_

[飞书用户 用户]: 📑 论文自检任务：附录内容引用完整性检查

请检查论文附录（Appendix）的所有内容是否在正文里都有准确、合理的引用。

AI 写作的高频问题：附录里塞了一堆补充材料（额外实验、超参表、prompt 模板、proof、failure case），但正文从头到尾没有一句话指向它们。这种"孤儿附录内容"对 reviewer 来说等于不存在 —— 既浪费篇幅，也让附录显得拼凑。

ACL 政策也明确：附录是 supplementary 性质的延伸，必须由正文驱动读者去看。完全没被引用的附录章节会被视为低质量补充材料。

---

自检要求：

1. 逐项盘点附录
   把附录里的每一个 section / subsection / table / figure / algorithm / proof / prompt 都列出来。常见类型：
   - Appendix A: Implementation details / Training details
   - Appendix B: Additional results / Ablations
   - Appendix C: Hyperparameter search
   - Appendix D: Prompt templates
   - Appendix E: Proofs
   - Appendix F: Failure cases / Error analysis / Qualitative examples
   - Appendix G: Dataset construction details

2. 逐项验证引用
   对每一项，在正文里搜：是否有至少一句话指向它？引用形式应该是：
   - "... (see Appendix A for full hyperparameters)"
   - "... we defer the proof to Appendix B"
   - "... Table 5 in Appendix C reports the breakdown"
   - "... Figure 7 (Appendix D) visualizes ..."

   不合格的"引用"：
   ❌ 只在 paper 末尾甩一句 "See appendix for more details"（没指向具体 section）
   ❌ 在 Limitations 或 Conclusion 才提一次（应该在内容相关的章节就引出）
   ❌ 引用了但没说明为什么读者要去看（"see Appendix B" 没下文）

3. 修复策略（按优先级）
   - 优选：在最相关的正文章节加一句自然的引用。例如训练细节 → 在 Experimental Setup 末尾："For full training details including learning rate schedule and gradient clipping, see Appendix A."
   - 次选：如果实在找不到合适位置插入引用，且该附录内容对核心 claim 不必要，直接删除附录内容（不要保留无引用的"装饰性附录"）
   - 不要做：在某个章节最后硬塞一行 "See Appendix X." 当作补丁——这不算合理引用

4. 引用质量原则
   每条引用应该让 reviewer 立刻明白：
   - 为什么他要去翻那里（解决什么疑问 / 验证什么 claim）
   - 能在那里找到什么（具体是表、图、proof 还是定性例子）

---

完成后请汇报：

(a) 附录有多少个 section / subsection？分别是什么内容？
(b) 哪些原本没有正文引用？现在加在了哪个章节哪一句话？
(c) 是否有附录内容被你判定为"无必要"而删除？删掉了哪些？
